\subsection{必要字段字典}
NULL表示该字段允许为空，NN表示非空，PK表示主键，FK表示外键，U表示单列唯一。复合唯一、时间排斥及跨表不变量在表后约束中给出。事件及财务事实保留来源，公共字段仍按表列明，避免在开发时遗漏。\par
\Needspace{639pt}
\subsubsection{门诊（clinics）}
保存ADC门诊位置、主管和营业信息。\par
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}{\textwidth}
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\captionof{table}{clinics字段}\label{tab452}
\begin{tabular}{@{}p{3.940cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{2.740cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{3.140cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.140cm}@{}}
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{字段} & \bfseries \Cell{类型} & \bfseries \Cell{键与空值} & \bfseries \Cell{含义及限制}\\\midrule
\Cell{id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；PK} & \Cell{稳定全局标识，不承载业务顺序}\\[1pt]
\Cell{clinic\_\allowbreak{}no} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{连锁内稳定门诊编号}\\[1pt]
\Cell{name} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{门诊名称}\\[1pt]
\Cell{address} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{邮政地址}\\[1pt]
\Cell{manager\_\allowbreak{}staff\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\\staff.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{负责本门诊的主管员工}\\[1pt]
\Cell{description} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NULL} & \Cell{对外展示的门诊简介}\\[1pt]
\Cell{opening\_\allowbreak{}hours} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NULL} & \Cell{面向患者展示的营业时间文字}\\[1pt]
\Cell{phone} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{门诊联系电话}\\[1pt]
\Cell{email} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{门诊联系邮箱}\\[1pt]
\Cell{floor\_\allowbreak{}area\_\allowbreak{}m2} & \Cell{NUMERIC(\allowbreak{}18,\allowbreak{}4)\allowbreak{}} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{办公面积，平方米}\\[1pt]
\Cell{time\_\allowbreak{}zone} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{业务时区，采用Asia/\allowbreak{}Shanghai}\\[1pt]
\Cell{opens\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIME} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{正常营业开始}\\[1pt]
\Cell{closes\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIME} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{正常营业结束}\\[1pt]
\Cell{status} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{preparing、active、suspended、closed}\\[1pt]
\Cell{opened\_\allowbreak{}on} & \Cell{DATE} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{门诊实际进入经营范围日期}\\[1pt]
\Cell{closed\_\allowbreak{}on} & \Cell{DATE} & \Cell{NULL} & \Cell{永久结束经营日期；仍经营为空}\\[1pt]
\Cell{created\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{创建时间，UTC保存}\\[1pt]
\Cell{updated\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{当前草稿或状态最后变更时间}\\[1pt]
\Cell{row\_\allowbreak{}version} & \Cell{INTEGER} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{并发版本，成功修改递增}\\[1pt]
\bottomrule\end{tabular}
}\end{minipage}\par\vspace{6pt}
约束：UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}clinic\_\allowbreak{}no)\allowbreak{}；row\_\allowbreak{}version>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}1；floor\_\allowbreak{}area\_\allowbreak{}m2>\allowbreak{}0；opens\_\allowbreak{}at<\allowbreak{}closes\_\allowbreak{}at；closed\_\allowbreak{}on IS NULL OR closed\_\allowbreak{}on>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}opened\_\allowbreak{}on；主管任职和manager权限须覆盖门诊及生效期间。停业门诊保留历史记录，并按登记范围提供季度所需的月度核对依据。\par
\Needspace{552pt}
\subsubsection{员工与医生档案（staff）}
员工档案保存稳定员工编号和可选唯一账号关联，医生与非医生共享员工主体。出生日期、家庭住址和电子邮箱用于内部人事管理；公开医生资料不返回这三项。\par
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{3.6cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{2.1cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{3.7cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.3cm}@{}}
\caption{staff字段}\label{schema_staff}\\
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{字段} & \bfseries \Cell{类型} & \bfseries \Cell{键与空值} & \bfseries \Cell{含义及限制}\\\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{字段} & \bfseries \Cell{类型} & \bfseries \Cell{键与空值} & \bfseries \Cell{含义及限制}\\\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；PK} & \Cell{稳定全局标识，自动生成UUID}\\[2pt]
\Cell{user\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；UNIQUE；FK→\allowbreak{}users.id} & \Cell{关联登录账号；staff.user\_\allowbreak{}id可空且唯一，岗位授权中必填}\\[2pt]
\Cell{employee\_\allowbreak{}no} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN；UNIQUE} & \Cell{唯一员工编号，跨门诊保持同一编号；公开医生DTO不返回}\\[2pt]
\Cell{name} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{员工姓名}\\[2pt]
\Cell{phone} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{内部人员联系电话；公开医生DTO不返回}\\[2pt]
\Cell{specialty} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{岗位或医生专长说明}\\[2pt]
\Cell{is\_\allowbreak{}dentist} & \Cell{BOOLEAN} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{是否牙医；支持非医生员工与医生采用同一员工主体}\\[2pt]
\Cell{active} & \Cell{BOOLEAN} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{当前可用标记；停用保留历史}\\[2pt]
\Cell{row\_\allowbreak{}version} & \Cell{INTEGER} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{乐观并发版本；成功状态变更递增}\\[2pt]
\Cell{is\_\allowbreak{}demo} & \Cell{BOOLEAN} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{合成演示资料标记；不接受普通表单伪造}\\[2pt]
\Cell{bio} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{对外展示的个人简介}\\[2pt]
\Cell{focus\_\allowbreak{}areas} & \Cell{TEXT[]} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{对外展示的专长项目数组}\\[2pt]
\Cell{birth\_\allowbreak{}date} & \Cell{DATE} & \Cell{NULL} & \Cell{内部人事出生日期；公开医生DTO不返回}\\[2pt]
\Cell{home\_\allowbreak{}address} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NULL} & \Cell{内部人事家庭住址；公开医生DTO不返回}\\[2pt]
\Cell{email} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NULL} & \Cell{内部人事电子邮箱；公开医生DTO不返回}\\[2pt]
\end{longtable}
}
约束：employee\_\allowbreak{}no唯一；user\_\allowbreak{}id可空且唯一，关联users.id。is\_\allowbreak{}dentist区分牙医和非医生员工；active停用不删除历史。row\_\allowbreak{}version为正整数。医生档案、非医生员工档案及账号授权分别由相应管理入口维护；制表和复核同时核对账号与员工编号。\par
\subsubsection{登录账号（users）}
账号负责认证，员工通过staff.user\_\allowbreak{}id关联本人账号；患者通过患者档案关联本人账号。员工号和账号名分别保持稳定唯一，显示姓名不用于推断数据权限。\par
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{3.6cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{2.1cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{3.7cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.3cm}@{}}
\caption{users字段}\label{schema_users}\\
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{字段} & \bfseries \Cell{类型} & \bfseries \Cell{键与空值} & \bfseries \Cell{含义及限制}\\\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{字段} & \bfseries \Cell{类型} & \bfseries \Cell{键与空值} & \bfseries \Cell{含义及限制}\\\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；PK} & \Cell{稳定全局标识，自动生成UUID}\\[2pt]
\Cell{username} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN；UNIQUE} & \Cell{小写规范化登录名，唯一}\\[2pt]
\Cell{password\_\allowbreak{}hash} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{不可逆安全密码散列，不保存明文密码}\\[2pt]
\Cell{display\_\allowbreak{}name} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{账号显示姓名，不作为员工身份关联依据}\\[2pt]
\Cell{active} & \Cell{BOOLEAN} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{当前可用标记；停用保留历史}\\[2pt]
\Cell{created\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{记录创建时刻，保存为带时区时间}\\[2pt]
\Cell{account\_\allowbreak{}version} & \Cell{INTEGER} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{账号并发与会话有效性版本，正整数}\\[2pt]
\Cell{must\_\allowbreak{}change\_\allowbreak{}password} & \Cell{BOOLEAN} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{首次或临时凭据是否必须更改密码}\\[2pt]
\end{longtable}
}
约束：username小写且唯一，active控制账号启停；account\_\allowbreak{}version为正整数。临时密码流程由must\_\allowbreak{}change\_\allowbreak{}password控制，停用与密码变更使旧会话失效，保留历史经办记录。\par
\subsubsection{岗位与门诊授权（role\_assignments）}
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{3.6cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{2.1cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{3.7cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.3cm}@{}}
\caption{role\_\allowbreak{}assignments字段}\label{schema_role_assignments}\\
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{字段} & \bfseries \Cell{类型} & \bfseries \Cell{键与空值} & \bfseries \Cell{含义及限制}\\\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{字段} & \bfseries \Cell{类型} & \bfseries \Cell{键与空值} & \bfseries \Cell{含义及限制}\\\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；PK} & \Cell{稳定全局标识，自动生成UUID}\\[2pt]
\Cell{user\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\allowbreak{}users.id} & \Cell{关联登录账号；staff.user\_\allowbreak{}id可空且唯一，岗位授权中必填}\\[2pt]
\Cell{role} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{front、doctor、manager、hq；同一账号可承担多岗位}\\[2pt]
\Cell{clinic\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\allowbreak{}clinics.id} & \Cell{来源门诊的稳定ID}\\[2pt]
\end{longtable}
}
约束：role取front、doctor、manager、hq；总部hq的clinic\_\allowbreak{}id必须为空，其他岗位必须绑定门诊。同(user\_\allowbreak{}id,role,clinic\_\allowbreak{}id)唯一，空值按同值处理。服务端从会话读取当前账号与岗位，每项操作再检查员工状态及门诊范围；独立复核同时比较账号和staff.id。\par

保存患者身份、联系资料和逐项过敏核对状态。\par

}
约束：row\_\allowbreak{}version>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}1；latest\_\allowbreak{}at>\allowbreak{}earliest\_\allowbreak{}at；号源释放仅触发邀请，不把候补直接记为预约成功。已有预约提前安排采用原子改约，不能留下双重占用。\par
\Needspace{216pt}
\subsubsection{服务通知任务（notification\_tasks）}
保证预约、改期、候补与随访通知有重试和人工接手。\par

}
约束：UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}dedupe\_\allowbreak{}key)\allowbreak{}；row\_\allowbreak{}version>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}1；attempt\_\allowbreak{}count>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}0；分别记录已发送、已送达和患者已读状态。发送失败后重试，由前台跟进。；notification\_\allowbreak{}tasks即服务通知outbox。预约或业务事件、通知任务及命令回执在同一事务提交；后台只发送已提交任务。；后台以FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED领取任务并记录租约后提交，再进行外部调用。租约到期可恢复，但已调用且结果不明时先按相同dedupe\_\allowbreak{}key查询或重试渠道幂等接口；结果不明时标记unknown，由前台核实患者是否收到。；每次尝试的渠道编号、时点和结果追加到受控记录；传输失败不撤销原业务。\par
\Needspace{650pt}

\Needspace{216pt}

记录患者或合法代理针对确切方案版本作出的决定。\par

}
约束：预约确认、登录点击和付款均不替代医疗知情决定。撤回以新记录保留原决定；方案变更后重新解释确认。；scope\_\allowbreak{}snapshot属于签署内容的不可变快照；每个项目标识须属于plan\_\allowbreak{}version\_\allowbreak{}id。实际执行项目必须在有效确认范围内，未包含项目不得因同版本其他项目已同意而放行。\par

\Cell{service\_\allowbreak{}price\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\\service\_\allowbreak{}prices.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{执行时适用价格版本}\\[1pt]

约束：UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}execution\_\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}line\_\allowbreak{}no)\allowbreak{}；UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}visit\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{}；line\_\allowbreak{}no>\allowbreak{}0；unit\_\allowbreak{}price\_\allowbreak{}snapshot\_\allowbreak{}cents>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}0；completed\_\allowbreak{}quantity>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}0；quantity\_\allowbreak{}scale\_\allowbreak{}snapshot BETWEEN 0 AND 4；(\allowbreak{}outcome=\allowbreak{}'not\_\allowbreak{}performed' AND completed\_\allowbreak{}quantity=\allowbreak{}0)\allowbreak{} OR (\allowbreak{}outcome IN (\allowbreak{}'completed',\allowbreak{}'partial')\allowbreak{} AND completed\_\allowbreak{}quantity>\allowbreak{}0)\allowbreak{}；visit\_\allowbreak{}id必须等于所属执行单的visit\_\allowbreak{}id；实际医生须是签署执行单的医生。只保存实际完成数量，未实施计划不作为收费或收入依据。价格快照从已确认报价及获准价格规则取得。；not\_\allowbreak{}performed只保留未实施结论和原因，不能进入结算单。部分执行仅就实际完成数量计费；实际医生姓名由冻结快照展示，业绩按稳定医生标识归集。；FOREIGN KEY(\allowbreak{}execution\_\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}visit\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{} REFERENCES treatment\_\allowbreak{}executions(\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}visit\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{}\par

约束：UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}clinic\_\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}material\_\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}lot\_\allowbreak{}no)\allowbreak{}；UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}source\_\allowbreak{}fingerprint,\allowbreak{}source\_\allowbreak{}line\_\allowbreak{}no)\allowbreak{}；row\_\allowbreak{}version>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}1；initial\_\allowbreak{}quantity>\allowbreak{}0；expires\_\allowbreak{}on IS NULL OR expires\_\allowbreak{}on>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}received\_\allowbreak{}on；source\_\allowbreak{}line\_\allowbreak{}no>\allowbreak{}0；total\_\allowbreak{}cost\_\allowbreak{}cents IS NULL OR total\_\allowbreak{}cost\_\allowbreak{}cents>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}0；cost\_\allowbreak{}status<\allowbreak{}>\allowbreak{}'verified' OR total\_\allowbreak{}cost\_\allowbreak{}cents IS NOT NULL；用品领用时登记实际耗用数量及成本，归入对应医生的季度成本。新领用品采用有效且可用的批次。\par
\Needspace{216pt}
\subsubsection{实际材料耗用与更正（material\_consumptions）}
将每项真实用品耗用唯一归到执行项目及实际医生。\par

\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；PK} & \Cell{稳定全局标识，不承载业务顺序}\\[1pt]
\Cell{execution\_\allowbreak{}item\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\\execution\_\allowbreak{}items.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{实际治疗项目}\\[1pt]

}
约束：quantity>\allowbreak{}0；(\allowbreak{}entry\_\allowbreak{}type=\allowbreak{}'consume' AND reverses\_\allowbreak{}consumption\_\allowbreak{}id IS NULL)\allowbreak{} OR (\allowbreak{}entry\_\allowbreak{}type=\allowbreak{}'reverse' AND reverses\_\allowbreak{}consumption\_\allowbreak{}id IS NOT NULL)\allowbreak{}；unit\_\allowbreak{}cost\_\allowbreak{}snapshot\_\allowbreak{}cents IS NULL OR unit\_\allowbreak{}cost\_\allowbreak{}snapshot\_\allowbreak{}cents>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}0；cost\_\allowbreak{}cents IS NULL OR cost\_\allowbreak{}cents>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}0；row\_\allowbreak{}version>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}1；cost\_\allowbreak{}status<\allowbreak{}>\allowbreak{}'priced' OR (\allowbreak{}cost\_\allowbreak{}cents IS NOT NULL AND unit\_\allowbreak{}cost\_\allowbreak{}snapshot\_\allowbreak{}cents IS NOT NULL AND priced\_\allowbreak{}by IS NOT NULL AND priced\_\allowbreak{}at IS NOT NULL)\allowbreak{}；批次门诊须与执行就诊门诊一致。净耗用不超过批次数量；冲回不超过原行未冲数量及成本。各次分配成本相加等于批次总成本，余下几分钱计入最后一份分配。医疗用品成本只从此表进入业绩，不再次记入运营费用。；实际执行签署先固定用品、数量、批次和责任；缺已核验成本时保留pending，不补0。后续获授权核价只补成本及证据，不覆盖已签数量。任何应纳入期间的pending耗用均阻断关账与正式季度计算。\par
\Needspace{540pt}

约束：UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}invoice\_\allowbreak{}no)\allowbreak{}；UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}visit\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{}；UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}visit\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{}；row\_\allowbreak{}version>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}1；original\_\allowbreak{}total\_\allowbreak{}cents>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}0；visit\_\allowbreak{}id NOT NULL UNIQUE。患者及门诊必须等于就诊；当前应收=\allowbreak{}原明细总额+\allowbreak{}全部有效调整，不另造第二张就诊结算单。预收独立登记，不虚造未实施治疗。；FOREIGN KEY(\allowbreak{}visit\_\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}patient\_\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}clinic\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{} REFERENCES visits(\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}patient\_\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}clinic\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{}\par
\Needspace{604pt}

约束：UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}invoice\_\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}line\_\allowbreak{}no)\allowbreak{}；UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}execution\_\allowbreak{}item\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{}；UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}invoice\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{}；line\_\allowbreak{}no>\allowbreak{}0；quantity>\allowbreak{}0；unit\_\allowbreak{}price\_\allowbreak{}cents>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}0；discount\_\allowbreak{}cents>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}0；line\_\allowbreak{}total\_\allowbreak{}cents=\allowbreak{}ROUND(\allowbreak{}quantity*\allowbreak{}unit\_\allowbreak{}price\_\allowbreak{}cents)\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}discount\_\allowbreak{}cents；line\_\allowbreak{}total\_\allowbreak{}cents>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}0；执行项目只能计费一次；名称、单位、数量和单价与签署执行快照一致，优惠单独留证据。开单冻结后不得覆盖，差错用调整单。；原执行必须已签且outcome为completed或partial，且completed\_\allowbreak{}quantity>\allowbreak{}0；数量精度满足其冻结规则。；FOREIGN KEY(\allowbreak{}invoice\_\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}visit\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{} REFERENCES invoices(\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}visit\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{}；FOREIGN KEY(\allowbreak{}execution\_\allowbreak{}item\_\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}visit\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{} REFERENCES execution\_\allowbreak{}items(\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}visit\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{}\par
\Needspace{574pt}

约束：UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}adjustment\_\allowbreak{}no)\allowbreak{}；UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}reverses\_\allowbreak{}adjustment\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{}；delta\_\allowbreak{}cents<\allowbreak{}>\allowbreak{}0；requested\_\allowbreak{}by<\allowbreak{}>\allowbreak{}approved\_\allowbreak{}by；调整后原费用行净应收不得为负。费用减免不自动退钱；退款也不自动减免应收。更正以前期间在当前开放期入账，保留原服务与原费用引用。以有效员工档案、不同账号ID和不同员工ID实施制审分离。；调减后如原净核销高于新应收，同事务释放超额核销到原款未分配预收；实际退款另有审批和渠道结果，不能留下无解释的超额核销。\par
\Needspace{216pt}

\Cell{entry\_\allowbreak{}type} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{apply或release}\\[1pt]
\Cell{amount\_\allowbreak{}cents} & \Cell{BIGINT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{核销或释放绝对金额；单位分}\\[1pt]
\Cell{reverses\_\allowbreak{}allocation\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\\payment\_\allowbreak{}allocations.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{释放时对应的原核销}\\[1pt]
\Cell{refund\_\allowbreak{}transaction\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\\refund\_\allowbreak{}transactions.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{因退款释放时的成功退付}\\[1pt]
\Cell{cross\_\allowbreak{}clinic\_\allowbreak{}authorization\_\allowbreak{}ref} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NULL} & \Cell{异店代收或余额使用授权依据}\\[1pt]
\Cell{created\_\allowbreak{}by} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\\users.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{核销账号}\\[1pt]
\Cell{allocated\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{核销或释放时间}\\[1pt]
\Cell{invoice\_\allowbreak{}line\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\\invoice\_\allowbreak{}lines.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{实际被核销的费用行}\\[1pt]
\Cell{invoice\_\allowbreak{}adjustment\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\\invoice\_\allowbreak{}adjustments.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{费用调减释放核销时引用的获批更正}\\[1pt]
\Cell{created\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{创建时间，UTC保存}\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}
}
约束：amount\_\allowbreak{}cents>\allowbreak{}0；(\allowbreak{}entry\_\allowbreak{}type=\allowbreak{}'apply' AND reverses\_\allowbreak{}allocation\_\allowbreak{}id IS NULL)\allowbreak{} OR (\allowbreak{}entry\_\allowbreak{}type=\allowbreak{}'release' AND reverses\_\allowbreak{}allocation\_\allowbreak{}id IS NOT NULL)\allowbreak{}；原款和结算单患者必须一致。成功原款-\allowbreak{}成功退款-\allowbreak{}净核销不得为负；单据净核销不超过当前应收。release不超过原核销未释放额。跨店分配保留服务店与收款店差额来源，形成内部往来，不产生第二次患者收款。；每行核销到结算单项目；项目净核销不超过其原额及有效调整后的应收。整单余额由各项目计算，不以患者总余额代替项目状态。；费用调减导致原核销超过新应收时，审批事务按确定顺序追加release将超额转回同患者预收；它不产生现金退款。释放引用原核销及invoice\_\allowbreak{}adjustment\_\allowbreak{}id。；release必须指向原apply且收款、结算单和项目一致，并说明退款成功或获批费用调整来源；同一来源释放不能超其尚未释放部分。；FOREIGN KEY(\allowbreak{}invoice\_\allowbreak{}line\_\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}invoice\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{} REFERENCES invoice\_\allowbreak{}lines(\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}invoice\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{}\par
\Needspace{216pt}

\Cell{updated\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{当前草稿或状态最后变更时间}\\*[1pt]

\end{longtable}
}
约束：UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}refund\_\allowbreak{}no)\allowbreak{}；row\_\allowbreak{}version>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}1；requested\_\allowbreak{}cents>\allowbreak{}0；approved\_\allowbreak{}cents>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}0 AND approved\_\allowbreak{}cents<\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}requested\_\allowbreak{}cents；approved\_\allowbreak{}by IS NULL OR approved\_\allowbreak{}by<\allowbreak{}>\allowbreak{}requested\_\allowbreak{}by；有效批准占用可退来源，成功和未决执行均占用额度。申请与审批校验不同账号及不同员工ID；已核销退款只释放其对应核销，费用是否减免另办。一个申请只针对一笔原款及一个核销来源或未分配余额。；预收可退余额=\allowbreak{}原成功收款-\allowbreak{}成功退款-\allowbreak{}当前净核销-\allowbreak{}有效未执行的预收退款冻结。已核销退款按原核销剩余可释放额扣除同来源有效冻结，不再重复扣入预收可用额。；成功退款同时减少原款净额；若退已核销款还同步release原核销，因此不会凭空增加患者预收。申请所指allocation\_\allowbreak{}id必须属于payment\_\allowbreak{}id且为原apply。\par
\Needspace{627pt}

}
约束：UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}document\_\allowbreak{}no)\allowbreak{}；UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}source\_\allowbreak{}fingerprint,\allowbreak{}source\_\allowbreak{}line\_\allowbreak{}no)\allowbreak{}；row\_\allowbreak{}version>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}1；signed\_\allowbreak{}amount\_\allowbreak{}cents<\allowbreak{}>\allowbreak{}0；(\allowbreak{}entry\_\allowbreak{}type=\allowbreak{}'expense' AND signed\_\allowbreak{}amount\_\allowbreak{}cents>\allowbreak{}0 AND reverses\_\allowbreak{}document\_\allowbreak{}id IS NULL)\allowbreak{} OR (\allowbreak{}entry\_\allowbreak{}type=\allowbreak{}'reverse' AND signed\_\allowbreak{}amount\_\allowbreak{}cents<\allowbreak{}0 AND reverses\_\allowbreak{}document\_\allowbreak{}id IS NOT NULL)\allowbreak{}；reviewed\_\allowbreak{}by IS NULL OR reviewed\_\allowbreak{}by<\allowbreak{}>\allowbreak{}prepared\_\allowbreak{}by；source\_\allowbreak{}line\_\allowbreak{}no>\allowbreak{}0；医疗用品的购买凭据不再作为运营费重复进入业绩。共享费用只按已核验实际工时分摊，不把排班计划当已工作事实。迟到更正计入当前开放期，保留原发生日期。；用品批次来源行与运营费来源行交叉校验分类；同一来源行不得同时作为材料取得成本和运营费用。\par

\Needspace{216pt}

\Cell{fund\_\allowbreak{}cents} & \Cell{BIGINT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{全体净利合计为正时提取的10\%，四舍五入至分}\\[2pt]
\Cell{clinic\_\allowbreak{}count} & \Cell{INTEGER} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{采用的全部登记门诊数量，必须大于零}\\[2pt]
\Cell{doctor\_\allowbreak{}count} & \Cell{INTEGER} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{季度中按员工ID去重后的医生数}\\[2pt]
\Cell{row\_\allowbreak{}version} & \Cell{INTEGER} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{乐观并发版本；成功状态变更递增}\\[2pt]
\Cell{created\_\allowbreak{}by} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\allowbreak{}users.id} & \Cell{创建或制表账号}\\[2pt]
\Cell{created\_\allowbreak{}staff\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\allowbreak{}staff.id} & \Cell{创建或制表时的有效员工身份}\\[2pt]
\Cell{submitted\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NULL} & \Cell{制表人提交季度草稿的时刻}\\[2pt]
\Cell{approved\_\allowbreak{}by} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\allowbreak{}users.id} & \Cell{独立总部批准账号}\\[2pt]
\Cell{approved\_\allowbreak{}staff\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\allowbreak{}staff.id} & \Cell{独立总部批准员工}\\[2pt]
\Cell{approved\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NULL} & \Cell{季度批准时刻}\\[2pt]
\Cell{void\_\allowbreak{}reason} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NULL} & \Cell{未批准快照作废的具体原因}\\[2pt]
\Cell{voided\_\allowbreak{}by} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\allowbreak{}users.id} & \Cell{作废经办账号}\\[2pt]
\Cell{voided\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NULL} & \Cell{作废时刻}\\[2pt]
\Cell{created\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{记录创建时刻，保存为带时区时间}\\[2pt]
\end{longtable}
}
约束：quarter符合YYYY-Qn；rule\_\allowbreak{}version固定为quarterly-positive-contribution-v1。status为draft、submitted、approved、voided；每季度仅一个非voided快照。净利等于三类合计相减，基金等于非负净利的10\%四舍五入至分。批准时账号、员工和时间齐全，且账号及员工分别不同于制表者；作废必须有原因、经办人和时间。\par

约束：联合主键(pool\_\allowbreak{}id,month\_\allowbreak{}id)，且(pool\_\allowbreak{}id,clinic\_\allowbreak{}id,month)唯一。source\_\allowbreak{}revision为正整数。固定采用时门诊、月份、总依据、零业务声明和关闭复核身份。append-only触发器禁止覆盖或删除；生成时必须覆盖全部登记门诊三个自然月。\par

}
约束：同(pool\_\allowbreak{}id,doctor\_\allowbreak{}id)唯一。weight\_\allowbreak{}cents=max(contribution\_\allowbreak{}cents,0)，amount\_\allowbreak{}cents非负，row\_\allowbreak{}version为正整数。最大余数法使全部个人金额严格等于fund\_\allowbreak{}cents；同余按员工编号、医生ID稳定排序。支付触发器更新版本；本人读取以会话员工ID筛选。\par

\Cell{resolved\_\allowbreak{}staff\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\allowbreak{}staff.id} & \Cell{实际核实付款结果的员工身份}\\[2pt]

}
约束：reference全局唯一且1—500字；result\_\allowbreak{}reference非空时同样为1—500字，两类凭证通过事务锁交叉去重。新记录必须pending；只可依据凭据从pending转succeeded或failed，终态及原始额度不可改写，记录禁止删除。触发器锁定approved份额，成功与pending合计不超额度；未知继续pending，明确失败释放占用。\par

按实际划转结果清偿收款店与服务店的内部余额。\par

\Cell{receiving\_\allowbreak{}clinic\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\\clinics.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{资金划入门诊}\\[1pt]
\Cell{amount\_\allowbreak{}cents} & \Cell{BIGINT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{本次划转金额；单位分}\\[1pt]
\Cell{prepared\_\allowbreak{}by} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\\users.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{制单账号}\\[1pt]
\Cell{approved\_\allowbreak{}by} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\\users.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{独立批准账号}\\[1pt]

\Cell{row\_\allowbreak{}version} & \Cell{INTEGER} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{并发版本，成功修改递增}\\[1pt]
\bottomrule\end{tabular}
}\end{minipage}\par\vspace{6pt}
约束：UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}settlement\_\allowbreak{}no)\allowbreak{}；UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}bank\_\allowbreak{}transaction\_\allowbreak{}ref)\allowbreak{}；row\_\allowbreak{}version>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}1；paying\_\allowbreak{}clinic\_\allowbreak{}id<\allowbreak{}>\allowbreak{}receiving\_\allowbreak{}clinic\_\allowbreak{}id；amount\_\allowbreak{}cents>\allowbreak{}0；approved\_\allowbreak{}by IS NULL OR approved\_\allowbreak{}by<\allowbreak{}>\allowbreak{}prepared\_\allowbreak{}by；默认同一经营主体内的获授权门诊。清算只减少内部往来，不新增患者收入、费用或集团收入；未知银行结果不减余额。\par
\Needspace{378pt}

逐笔把成功或未决清算定位到跨店核销及退款释放。\par

\Cell{amount\_\allowbreak{}cents} & \Cell{BIGINT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{本次冲抵金额；单位分}\\[1pt]

}\end{minipage}\par\vspace{6pt}
约束：UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}settlement\_\allowbreak{}id,\allowbreak{}payment\_\allowbreak{}allocation\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{}；amount\_\allowbreak{}cents>\allowbreak{}0；清算项合计等于清算金额，不能超过来源未清余额。原核销和退款释放可能形成相反方向往来，双方门诊应与清算方向一致。成功及未决清算均预占来源余额，失败释放。\par

\Cell{receiving\_\allowbreak{}dentist\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\\staff.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{接续医生}\\[1pt]
\Cell{handover\_\allowbreak{}status} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{none、pending、accepted、returned}\\[1pt]

}
约束：row\_\allowbreak{}version>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}1；latest\_\allowbreak{}on IS NULL OR latest\_\allowbreak{}on>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}earliest\_\allowbreak{}on；跨店复诊先由患者选择门诊和时间，再由前台核对资料、医生确认交接内容，随后确认新预约。接收医生在交接记录中填写接受结果；患者到店后登记接诊。费用核对人员查看账单及相应治疗明细。\par

\Cell{actor\_\allowbreak{}user\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\\users.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{用户命令的已认证账号；受信后台任务可空}\\[1pt]

\Cell{status} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{processing、committed、rejected}\\[1pt]

\bottomrule\end{tabular}
}\end{minipage}\par\vspace{6pt}
约束：UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}actor\_\allowbreak{}key,\allowbreak{}request\_\allowbreak{}id)\allowbreak{}；认证与当前权限检查先于回执读取。actor\_\allowbreak{}key由服务器从已认证主体生成，客户端不能指定其他人的回执范围。；同键且payload\_\allowbreak{}hash相同，返回原committed或rejected决策；同键不同内容、对象、操作类型或期望版本拒绝REQUEST\_\allowbreak{}KEY\_\allowbreak{}REUSED，不执行业务。；本地命令在一个短事务内插入processing、锁定聚合、校验并写业务，再写最终回执并提交；processing不单独提前提交。并发同键由唯一键及行锁串行化。；进程在提交前崩溃时，数据库回滚回执及业务，重试可重新办理；已提交但响应丢失时，重试取得原回执。仍持有事务锁时等待或返回可重试的处理中，不能另起新业务。；外部支付或通知不纳入本地长事务。命令提交的是accepted/\allowbreak{}pending业务状态、最终命令回执和outbox；实际渠道成功由可信事件另行推进。原回执的“已受理”不伪装成资金成功。；校验拒绝可在无业务变更的短事务中记录rejected决策；数据库中断等暂态失败整体回滚。回执保留期覆盖业务重试及资金核对周期，删除前不得导致同一业务流水可重复入账。\par
\Needspace{208pt}

\Cell{service\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\\services.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{明确项目；主诉待评估时可空}\\[1pt]
\Cell{chief\_\allowbreak{}complaint} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{原始主诉与特殊需求}\\[1pt]
\Cell{earliest\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{可接受时间范围开始}\\[1pt]
\Cell{latest\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{可接受时间范围结束}\\[1pt]
\Cell{source\_\allowbreak{}channel} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{online、phone、walk\_\allowbreak{}in、followup}\\[1pt]
\Cell{created\_\allowbreak{}by} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\\users.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{本人或代办账号}\\[1pt]
\Cell{owner\_\allowbreak{}user\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\\users.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{待处理前台或临床任务账号}\\[1pt]
\Cell{intent\_\allowbreak{}key} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{患者、项目或主诉与时间意愿的规范去重键}\\[1pt]
\Cell{status} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{pending、needs\_\allowbreak{}review、ready、scheduled、withdrawn}\\[1pt]
\Cell{review\_\allowbreak{}result} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NULL} & \Cell{医学评估或前台联系结论}\\[1pt]
\Cell{scheduled\_\allowbreak{}appointment\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\\appointments.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{最终确认预约}\\[1pt]
\Cell{created\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{提交时间，UTC保存}\\[1pt]
\Cell{updated\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{最后处理时间}\\[1pt]
\Cell{row\_\allowbreak{}version} & \Cell{INTEGER} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{并发版本}\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}
}
约束：UNIQUE(\allowbreak{}request\_\allowbreak{}no)\allowbreak{}；latest\_\allowbreak{}at>\allowbreak{}earliest\_\allowbreak{}at；row\_\allowbreak{}version>\allowbreak{}=\allowbreak{}1；申请保存患者希望的门诊和时间，选定实际时段并确认后建立预约；初诊评估按所选项目、医生班次及可用时段安排。；needs\_\allowbreak{}review由接诊医生核对患者需求后，给出评估项目或联系安排。；已满足申请与对应候补通过同一申请及intent\_\allowbreak{}key去重；查询时按同一申请合并展示候补及确认结果。；同一患者已有相同待处理需求时，提示继续办理原申请；患者确认另有新需求时分别保存。\par
\Needspace{592pt}
\subsubsection{统一资源占用（resource\_reservations）}
统一记录临时占位与正式预约对患者、医生和牙椅的资源占用。占位单和预约单分别以唯一的reservation\_\allowbreak{}id关联同一条资源记录；保留的患者、医生、门诊、项目和时段业务快照应与该记录一致。\par
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}{\textwidth}
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\captionof{table}{resource\_reservations字段}\label{tab688}
\begin{tabular}{@{}p{3.940cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{2.740cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{3.140cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.140cm}@{}}
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{字段} & \bfseries \Cell{类型} & \bfseries \Cell{键与空值} & \bfseries \Cell{含义及限制}\\\midrule
\Cell{id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；PK} & \Cell{资源占用记录标识}\\[1pt]
\Cell{patient\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\\patients.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{实际就诊患者}\\[1pt]
\Cell{clinic\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\\clinics.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{服务门诊}\\[1pt]
\Cell{service\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\\services.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{预约服务}\\[1pt]
\Cell{doctor\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；FK→\\staff.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{接诊医生}\\[1pt]
\Cell{chair\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；复合FK} & \Cell{牙椅，与门诊共同限定归属}\\[1pt]
\Cell{shift\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；复合FK} & \Cell{医生班次，与门诊、医生共同限定归属}\\[1pt]
\Cell{starts\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{服务开始时间}\\[1pt]
\Cell{ends\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{服务实际结束时间}\\[1pt]
\Cell{occupied\_\allowbreak{}until} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{含准备或清理缓冲的资源占用结束时间}\\[1pt]
\Cell{status} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{held、booked、released、expired}\\[1pt]
\Cell{expires\_\allowbreak{}at} & \Cell{TIMESTAMPTZ} & \Cell{NULL} & \Cell{临时占位到期时间；held时必填}\\[1pt]
\bottomrule\end{tabular}
}\end{minipage}\par\vspace{6pt}
约束：服务结束晚于开始，占用结束不早于服务结束；牙椅与门诊、班次与门诊及医生分别以复合外键约束。对held和booked记录，以[starts\_\allowbreak{}at,\allowbreak{} occupied\_\allowbreak{}until)\allowbreak{}为左闭右开区间，分别对同一医生、同一牙椅和同一患者设置时间排斥约束。到期占位在写事务中标记expired；确认、取消或改期的资源状态与业务记录在同一事务中保持一致。\par
\Needspace{506pt}
\subsubsection{治疗价格参考（treatment\_references）}
记录面向患者展示的治疗项目参考区间、单位、年份和资料来源，便于按类别查询并了解费用组成；可关联评估或咨询服务，引导患者查看可预约时段。患者可用参考区间了解费用，医生面诊后确认实际治疗项目，结算单列出相应数量和价格。\par
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}{\textwidth}
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\captionof{table}{treatment\_references字段}\label{tab692}
\begin{tabular}{@{}p{3.940cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{2.740cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{3.140cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.140cm}@{}}
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{字段} & \bfseries \Cell{类型} & \bfseries \Cell{键与空值} & \bfseries \Cell{含义及限制}\\\midrule
\Cell{id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NN；PK} & \Cell{参考项目标识}\\[1pt]
\Cell{reference\_\allowbreak{}code} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN；U} & \Cell{稳定参考项目编码}\\[1pt]
\Cell{category} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{项目类别}\\[1pt]
\Cell{name} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{项目名称}\\[1pt]
\Cell{price\_\allowbreak{}min\_\allowbreak{}cents} & \Cell{INTEGER} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{参考价格下限，单位分，不小于零}\\[1pt]
\Cell{price\_\allowbreak{}max\_\allowbreak{}cents} & \Cell{INTEGER} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{参考价格上限，单位分，不小于下限}\\[1pt]
\Cell{unit} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{计价单位：次、颗或疗程}\\[1pt]
\Cell{reference\_\allowbreak{}year} & \Cell{INTEGER} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{参考资料年份}\\[1pt]
\Cell{source\_\allowbreak{}label} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{非空的资料来源标识}\\[1pt]
\Cell{description} & \Cell{TEXT} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{参考范围与费用说明}\\[1pt]
\Cell{booking\_\allowbreak{}service\_\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{UUID} & \Cell{NULL；FK→\\services.\allowbreak{}id} & \Cell{可关联的评估或咨询服务}\\[1pt]
\Cell{sort\_\allowbreak{}order} & \Cell{INTEGER} & \Cell{NN} & \Cell{展示顺序，不小于零}\\[1pt]
\bottomrule\end{tabular}
}\end{minipage}\par\vspace{6pt}
约束：参考编码唯一；价格上下限以整数分保存，上限不小于下限；参考年份、来源、单位和解释随记录一同展示。关联服务失效或删除时可保留参考资料而清空关联，关联项目不保证存在可预约号源。\par
\Needspace{297pt}
\subsection{预约服务的物理关系}\label{sec_booking_physical}
演示环境按数据库迁移001至009建立表。第7章前述概念和关系模型为完整业务设计；演示环境的核心关系以下表为准。预约、患者与经营表共用门诊、员工及患者编号。\par
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{2.94cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{7.14cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.04cm}@{}}
\caption{演示环境的数据表与来源}\label{tab_delivered_tables}\\
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 领域} & \Cell{\bfseries 迁移中的关键关系} & \Cell{\bfseries 使用规则}\\\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 领域} & \Cell{\bfseries 迁移中的关键关系} & \Cell{\bfseries 使用规则}\\\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{会话与组织} & \Cell{{users}、{sessions}、{role\_\allowbreak{}assignments}、{staff}、{clinics}} & \Cell{员工岗位以门诊授权定范围，员工与患者共享账号机制。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{资源与预约} & \Cell{{doctor\_\allowbreak{}shifts}、{doctor\_\allowbreak{}shift\_\allowbreak{}services}、{chair\_\allowbreak{}blocks}、{resource\_\allowbreak{}reservations}、{booking\_\allowbreak{}holds}、{appointments}、{appointment\_\allowbreak{}events}、{command\_\allowbreak{}receipts}} & \Cell{班次发布后派生号源；预约通过唯一占用和占位关联，改期通过旧预约外键串联。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{患者资料} & \Cell{{patients}、{patient\_\allowbreak{}allergies}、{patient\_\allowbreak{}clinic\_\allowbreak{}access}、{patient\_\allowbreak{}privacy\_\allowbreak{}consents}、{patient\_\allowbreak{}registration\_\allowbreak{}invites}、{patient\_\allowbreak{}avatars}} & \Cell{过敏项存于患者关联行；绑定、授权及头像使用同一患者主键。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{公开价格} & \Cell{{services}、{clinic\_\allowbreak{}services}、{treatment\_\allowbreak{}references}} & \Cell{预约项目参考费取服务目录；独立价格区间保留单位、年份和来源。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{月度与季度} & \Cell{{incentive\_\allowbreak{}months}、{doctor\_\allowbreak{}financial\_\allowbreak{}sources}、{incentive\_\allowbreak{}pools}、{incentive\_\allowbreak{}pool\_\allowbreak{}sources}、{doctor\_\allowbreak{}quarter\_\allowbreak{}results}、{incentive\_\allowbreak{}allocations}、{incentive\_\allowbreak{}payments}} & \Cell{按关闭版本汇总，保存季度来源与医生额度，发放记录关联批准份额。}\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}
}
患者临时占位与正式预约共用资源占用表。占位创建一条占用记录，确认后将该记录转为正式占用并关联唯一预约。医生、牙椅和患者分别使用同一组区间约束，比较区间为从开始时刻到含缓冲的占用截止时刻，右端点开放。取消或改期释放旧占用；到期占位先标为过期，再重新判断号源。\par
在预约服务中，预约单保存业务编号、预约状态和服务快照，通过唯一外键读取门诊、医生、牙椅和时段。班次与门诊、医生使用组合外键，牙椅与门诊使用组合外键。预约事件记录操作者、原因及替代关系，命令回执以操作者和命令标识为键。\par
门诊档案访问记录保存授权范围和创建账号。图\ref{fig_patient_privacy_relations}给出患者档案与各记录的键和数量关系。\par

\end{longtable}
}
号源查询在SQL中按门诊、项目、日期范围及可选医生筛选，结合已发布班次、项目时长、牙椅及已有预约计算空闲时段。患者历史按患者编号与时间分页；季度业绩按已核对的月度账证汇总，并保存来源版本。查询索引通过固定数据规模和执行计划检查。\par
\Needspace{9\baselineskip}
\Needspace{634pt}
激励月度查询利用incentive\_\allowbreak{}month\_\allowbreak{}period(month,clinic\_\allowbreak{}id,status)；季度有效版本由incentive\_\allowbreak{}one\_\allowbreak{}live\_\allowbreak{}quarter部分唯一索引保证；来源引用检索使用incentive\_\allowbreak{}sources\_\allowbreak{}month(month\_\allowbreak{}id,pool\_\allowbreak{}id)，付款汇总使用incentive\_\allowbreak{}payments\_\allowbreak{}allocation(allocation\_\allowbreak{}id,status)。创建、关闭、重开及季度状态变更使用同一期间事务序列锁；付款触发器锁定个人份额并核对批准状态与累计占用。\par
\section{业务逻辑、接口与表示层}
\Needspace{3\baselineskip}
\subsection{交付范围与业务衔接}\label{sec_delivery_scope}
课程演示由品牌官网、患者小程序和员工工作台组成。官网承接浏览与微信扫码，小程序办理本人预约，员工工作台发布排班、办理患者服务并核算季度业绩。第8章的页面与接口按表\ref{tab_delivery_scope}落地。第3至7章保留完整业务模型，为后续接诊与结算定义记录、职责和约束。\par
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{3.04cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.84cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{6.24cm}@{}}
\caption{三端交付与业务模型的衔接}\label{tab_delivery_scope}\\
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 业务环节} & \Cell{\bfseries 课程演示的交付设计} & \Cell{\bfseries 完整业务模型的接续}\\\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 业务环节} & \Cell{\bfseries 课程演示的交付设计} & \Cell{\bfseries 完整业务模型的接续}\\\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{公开入口} & \Cell{官网与小程序读取同一门诊、医生和服务目录。官网预约按钮跳至小程序码。} & \Cell{患者进入小程序预约，电话及现场需求由员工代约。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{预约与患者资料} & \Cell{交付UC-01至UC-07、UC-24及预约清单。预约状态为已确认、已取消、已改期。} & \Cell{UC-08、UC-09及UC-23的到诊部分接续预约结果，处理候补、提醒、到诊与未到统计。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{诊疗与结算} & \Cell{预约详情展示项目及预约参考费用。复诊入口按原预约带入门诊、项目和医生，重新查询时段。} & \Cell{UC-10至UC-18接续实际就诊，生成治疗记录、结算单、资金和日结记录。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{季度业绩与激励} & \Cell{交付UC-19至UC-22。月度底稿录入已归属医生的收入、用品成本和运营费用，逐项保留来源编号。} & \Cell{后续诊疗结算模块向月度底稿提供已核对的来源；季度继续按已关闭月度版本核算。}\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}
}
\subsection{总体架构与技术选择}
\begin{figure}[H]
\centering
\setlength{\fboxsep}{8pt}
\begin{tabular}{@{}ccc@{}}
\fbox{\parbox[c][34pt][c]{0.26\textwidth}{\centering\bfseries 品牌官网\\公开目录与预约指引}} &

$\downarrow$ & $\downarrow$ & $\downarrow$\\[5pt]
\multicolumn{3}{c}{\fbox{\parbox{0.88\textwidth}{\centering HTTPS 应用接口\\公开查询、会话与授权、预约事务、经营服务}}}\\[5pt]
\multicolumn{3}{c}{$\downarrow$}\\[5pt]
\multicolumn{3}{c}{\fbox{\parbox{0.88\textwidth}{\centering PostgreSQL\\门诊与医生目录、患者资料、资源占用、预约和经营账证}}}

\caption{品牌官网、小程序和员工工作台的分层架构}\label{architecture}
\end{figure}
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}{\textwidth}
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\captionof{table}{分层架构}\label{tab701}
\begin{tabular}{@{}p{2.540cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{8.240cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{4.340cm}@{}}
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{层} & \bfseries \Cell{职责与技术选择} & \bfseries \Cell{依赖方向}\\\midrule
\Cell{官网表示层} & \Cell{Vue 3 展示诊疗项目、医生、门诊环境与联系方式，提供小程序预约指引和员工登录入口} & \Cell{读取公开门诊、医生与服务目录。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{患者表示层} & \Cell{微信原生小程序与Type\allowbreak{}Script，使用Vant Weapp日历和自定义步骤条组织预约} & \Cell{公开目录供访客浏览，患者会话用于本人资料和预约。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{员工表示层} & \Cell{Vue 3、Type\allowbreak{}Script与Element Plus组件化后台；按前台、医生、店长和总部授权呈现工作台} & \Cell{页面调用独立API模块，表单状态与业务数据分开。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{应用层} & \Cell{Node.\allowbreak{}js与Type\allowbreak{}Script模块化服务，使用Fastify组织HTTP接口、认证、命令和查询} & \Cell{调用领域服务与仓储接口，不把校验复制到每个页面。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{领域层} & \Cell{身份与组织、预约、诊疗、结算、经营核算与激励} & \Cell{领域对象维护不变量，跨聚合规则由领域服务协调。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{基础设施层} & \Cell{Postgre\allowbreak{}SQL、数据映射器、对象存储、异步任务和外部渠道适配器} & \Cell{实现仓储及消息端口，外部细节不渗入领域规则。}\\[1pt]
\bottomrule\end{tabular}
}\end{minipage}\par\vspace{6pt}
系统采用模块化单体和统一事务数据库，面向约30家门诊的规划规模。两端的预约写入使用同一事务服务，医生、患者和牙椅的占用由同一数据库约束。通知和报表导出通过后台任务处理；缓存仅用于可重建的读取结果，预约可用性及资金余额最终以事务数据库为准。\par
\Needspace{378pt}
\subsection{领域边界与事务}
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}{\textwidth}
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\captionof{table}{聚合与跨模块联系}\label{tab704}
\begin{tabular}{@{}p{2.640cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{7.340cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.140cm}@{}}
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{领域} & \bfseries \Cell{聚合根与主要不变量} & \bfseries \Cell{跨模块交接}\\\midrule
\Cell{身份与组织} & \Cell{患者、员工、门诊；稳定身份、医生档案及授权范围} & \Cell{向预约和诊疗提供所需身份及能力，不暴露无关人事信息。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{预约} & \Cell{预约及资源预留；医生与牙椅不得冲突} & \Cell{确认事件驱动提醒；到诊后建立就诊。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{诊疗} & \Cell{就诊、治疗计划、执行；确认先于新增执行，签署后追加附记} & \Cell{已签署执行为结算与用品成本提供来源。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{结算} & \Cell{唯一就诊单、收款、分配及退款；金额合计一致和原单追溯} & \Cell{成功资金结果进入日结，服务收入进入经营核算。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{经营核算} & \Cell{成本来源、分摊和期间；来源不重复、归属可解释} & \Cell{关闭来源版本供医生季度结果使用。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{激励} & \Cell{季度结果、基金与个人分配；固定10\%基数及分配合计等于基金} & \Cell{独立批准后才能登记发放，登记奖金发放凭据。}\\[1pt]
\bottomrule\end{tabular}
}\end{minipage}\par\vspace{6pt}
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{2.440cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{3.840cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.140cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{3.540cm}@{}}
\caption{关键事务与失败恢复}\label{tab705}\\
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{操作} & \bfseries \Cell{受控范围} & \bfseries \Cell{锁与不变量} & \bfseries \Cell{失败恢复}\\\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{操作} & \bfseries \Cell{受控范围} & \bfseries \Cell{锁与不变量} & \bfseries \Cell{失败恢复}\\\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{发布医生跨店班次} & \Cell{doctor\_\allowbreak{}shifts及审计} & \Cell{医生行；检查医生在岗及门诊授权，数据库排斥约束检查时段重叠；同一医生全连锁有效班次不得时间重叠，区间按[开始,\allowbreak{}结束)\allowbreak{}处理} & \Cell{整体回滚，原班次及已约安排保持；资源变化另产生协调任务。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{号源占位与正式确认} & \Cell{resource\_\allowbreak{}reservations、booking\_\allowbreak{}holds、appointments、appointment\_\allowbreak{}events} & \Cell{按稳定编号顺序锁患者、医生、牙椅，再读班次、停用、占位及预约；将到期占位标为过期；患者、医生和牙椅在统一占用表中分别约束；同一事务确认全部资源；确认时复核开放项目、班次、时长与剩余资源} & \Cell{确认失败则回滚本次预约；占位到期后返回重新选时段。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{改约与候补补位} & \Cell{新旧预约、占位、事件及通知} & \Cell{旧预约版本及新旧医生牙椅，按统一顺序取得；新约创建与旧约标记rescheduled同事务；候补接受前不确认} & \Cell{新资源失败则保留旧约和资金归属；通知失败不回滚成功改约。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{医生签署实际治疗及计费} & \Cell{执行单、执行项目、临床记录、唯一结算单及明细} & \Cell{就诊、适用方案版本及执行；唯一visit\_\allowbreak{}id与execution\_\allowbreak{}item\_\allowbreak{}id限制重复；患者、就诊、接诊医生、牙位及患者确认内容一致；费用仅来自实际完成数量；一就诊一个结算单身份} & \Cell{缺临床条件不签署；重复请求返回同一已存在单据，数据库冲突不产生第二张账。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{部分付款及跨店核销} & \Cell{成功收款、payment\_\allowbreak{}allocations及审计} & \Cell{原款、涉及结算单按编号排序；重算成功退款和已批准冻结；净核销不超净原款和净应收；患者一致，跨店具备授权且保留往来来源} & \Cell{金额或范围不符整批回滚，既有核销和预收不被覆盖。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{费用减免与退款} & \Cell{费用调整独立提交；退款批准与实际执行分别提交} & \Cell{调整锁结算单；批准锁原款及原核销；成功回执锁申请、退付和需释放的核销；获批冻结及成功未决退付不超来源；退款成功与核销释放原子完成；费用调整不改变现金事实} & \Cell{外部失败或未知保留待查，不能重发新交易或假记成功；拒绝审批不改变原款。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{实际材料成本归属} & \Cell{材料批次、耗用与冲回行} & \Cell{来源批次及原耗用行；数量和分值不超来源，余下几分钱计入最后一份；用品成本按耗用来源单独核算} & \Cell{实际已用但缺成本依据时保存待补记录，核价后归集成本；更正追加反向记录。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{运营费分摊} & \Cell{费用凭据及全部cost\_\allowbreak{}allocations} & \Cell{费用凭据、核验工时快照；审批校验不同账号及不同员工ID；直接归属有明确医生；共同费用按已核验工时比例分摊；分配合计等于来源分值} & \Cell{工时依据、凭据编号和分配合计核对一致后，整单批准。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{跨店内部清算} & \Cell{清算单、来源分配和外部事件} & \Cell{按门诊对及来源核销编号锁定未清余额；成功和未决指令不超未清来源；只有核实成功才减少内部往来} & \Cell{银行未知保留预占并查询原指令；失败释放，不新增患者收入。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{激励月度底稿与修订} & \Cell{incentive\_\allowbreak{}months、doctor\_\allowbreak{}financial\_\allowbreak{}sources} & \Cell{期间事务序列锁及月份行锁；版本校验、不同账号与员工ID、医生覆盖和账证完整；源行修订追加，已引用月份保持锁定} & \Cell{冲突或缺项整体回滚；总部仅可重开无有效季度引用的月份，重开原因进入审计。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{季度业绩及10\%激励} & \Cell{基金、关闭来源、医生门诊结果及个人份额} & \Cell{全部登记门诊×三个自然月完整关闭；冻结source\_\allowbreak{}revision；先合计正负净利再取10\%，跨店后按正贡献分配至分，份额合计等于基金} & \Cell{缺项即拒绝。未批准且有误快照作废后重算；已批准来源保持锁定。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{激励实际发放} & \Cell{incentive\_\allowbreak{}payments及获批份额} & \Cell{支付触发器锁定份额并核对approved；唯一凭证交叉去重，成功与pending合计不得超过amount\_\allowbreak{}cents} & \Cell{未知保持pending，明确failed才释放；终态与原始额度不可改写，记录不可删除。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{写命令回执和通知outbox} & \Cell{command\_\allowbreak{}receipts、用例聚合、audit\_\allowbreak{}events及notification\_\allowbreak{}tasks/\allowbreak{}资金发件事件} & \Cell{先认证和授权，按actor\_\allowbreak{}key/\allowbreak{}request\_\allowbreak{}id取得唯一命令；随后按用例的固定聚合顺序加锁；业务变更、最终命令决策和待发任务一次提交；同键不同内容拒绝，重复同内容只重放原决策} & \Cell{提交前崩溃整体回滚；已提交响应丢失重放回执。外部调用在事务外，由租约、重试、验真回执与人工未知队列恢复，按渠道凭据核实最终结果。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{预约意向与资源承诺衔接} & \Cell{appointment\_\allowbreak{}requests、候补及最终预约} & \Cell{申请及其有效候补；确认资源仍按固定患者医生牙椅顺序锁定；申请和邀约不占作正式承诺；同意图满足后同步关联最终预约，需求统计沿来源去重} & \Cell{未匹配保留待处理状态，不生成虚假预约；资源失败不丢原意向。}\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}
}
Postgre\allowbreak{}SQL时间范围与排斥约束用于同一资源区间冲突，数据库唯一约束保证一就诊一单。款项分配、费用分摊和激励额度由事务中的行锁与合计校验共同维护；对高冲突操作采用行锁或可串行化事务并处理重试。事务失败时回滚本次操作，并向调用者说明冲突和可重试步骤。[R8]\par
\Needspace{412pt}

\subsection{公共接口契约}
接口统一使用 HTTPS 和路径前缀 {/api/v1}。登录提交账号、密码及入口类型 {staff} 或 {patient}，服务端核对入口与岗位后返回会话令牌、到期时间与身份。受保护请求在 Authorization 中携带 Bearer 令牌。\par
表\ref{tab709}给出用例与接口的对应关系，模块接续顺序见表\ref{tab_delivery_scope}。预约清单分别按患者本人和员工权限查询。\par
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}{\textwidth}
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\captionof{table}{接口公共约定}\label{tab708}
\begin{tabular}{@{}p{2.940cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{12.340cm}@{}}
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{内容} & \bfseries \Cell{设计}\\\midrule
\Cell{命令标识} & \Cell{预约确认、取消和改期请求携带 Idempotency-\allowbreak{}Key；同一身份、同一键及相同请求返回原结果，内容不同时拒绝。需防止覆盖的更新提交 expected\allowbreak{}Version 或 hold\allowbreak{}Version。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{权限} & \Cell{服务端从会话解析患者身份、员工角色和门诊范围；患者访问本人预约，员工按岗位授权办理，并记录实际操作者和就诊人。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{错误结构} & \Cell{成功响应包含 data 和 request\allowbreak{}Id；错误响应包含 error.\allowbreak{}code、error.\allowbreak{}message、可选 details 和 request\allowbreak{}Id，区分权限、资料、资源冲突和状态错误。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{分页} & \Cell{患者与员工预约列表按 page、page\allowbreak{}Size 查询，返回 items、total、page 和 page\allowbreak{}Size；按稳定条件筛选并明确当前查询范围。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{异步回调} & \Cell{验证渠道签名、金额、币种、商户和原交易关系；重复事件按唯一事件号去重，乱序结果按合法状态处理。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{日志} & \Cell{记录操作及业务标识，不记录密码、完整支付凭证或不必要的敏感病史。审计与可观测日志职责分开。}\\[1pt]
\bottomrule\end{tabular}
}\end{minipage}\par\vspace{6pt}

\caption{用例到接口的追踪（交付接口及接续契约）}\label{tab709}\\
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{用例} & \bfseries \Cell{接口设计}\\\midrule
\endfirsthead

\bfseries \Cell{用例} & \bfseries \Cell{接口设计}\\\midrule

\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{\mbox{UC-01}} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}auth/\allowbreak{}register 新建账号和档案。前台通过 GET、POST /\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}patients 核对或建档，通过 POST /\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}patients/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}invitations 发凭证；POST /\allowbreak{}auth/\allowbreak{}register-linked 消耗凭证并绑定原档案。本人资料、头像及隐私接口见表\ref{tab_patient_account_api}。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-02}} & \Cell{GET /\allowbreak{}doctors、GET /\allowbreak{}qualifications 查询医生与资格；POST、PUT /\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}doctors 和 /\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}qualifications 维护授权范围内的医生与资格资料。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-03}} & \Cell{GET /\allowbreak{}services 查询服务；POST、PUT /\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}services 维护项目、时长、费用与介绍；GET /\allowbreak{}treatment-\allowbreak{}references 提供独立的治疗参考价。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-04}} & \Cell{GET /\allowbreak{}shifts 查询排班；POST /\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}shifts 发布班次，POST /\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}shifts/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}cancel 撤销班次。服务端校验医生在岗、门诊授权、班次时间及跨店转场间隔。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-05}} & \Cell{GET /\allowbreak{}booking-\allowbreak{}policy 返回规则与日期查询范围；GET /\allowbreak{}booking-\allowbreak{}slots 按 clinic\allowbreak{}Id、service\allowbreak{}Id、date 及可选 doctor\allowbreak{}Id 查询候选时段。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-06}} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}booking-\allowbreak{}holds 取得临时占位；POST /\allowbreak{}appointments/\allowbreak{}confirm 携带 hold\allowbreak{}Id、hold\allowbreak{}Version、就诊人及规则确认标记和 Idempotency-\allowbreak{}Key 确认；GET /\allowbreak{}appointments/\allowbreak{}\{id\} 查询结果。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-07}} & \Cell{改期先通过 POST /\allowbreak{}booking-\allowbreak{}holds 取得新占位；POST /\allowbreak{}appointments/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}changes 携带 intent、reason、expected\allowbreak{}Version 和 Idempotency-\allowbreak{}Key，改期另传 hold\allowbreak{}Id、hold\allowbreak{}Version。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-08}} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}contact-\allowbreak{}tasks；POST /\allowbreak{}waitlist-\allowbreak{}invitations/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}responses；回执入口校验来源与事件唯一号。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-09}} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}appointments/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}attendance；POST /\allowbreak{}attendance-\allowbreak{}events/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}corrections。更正带证据、理由与版本。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-10}} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}visits/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}assessments；POST /\allowbreak{}treatment-\allowbreak{}plans；POST /\allowbreak{}treatment-\allowbreak{}plans/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}versions。签署人由当前医生账号及接诊记录确定。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-11}} & \Cell{GET /\allowbreak{}plan-\allowbreak{}versions/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}explanation；POST /\allowbreak{}plan-\allowbreak{}versions/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}consents，带scope、decision、evidence\allowbreak{}Ref及幂等键。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-12}} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}visits/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}executions；POST /\allowbreak{}executions/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}signatures；POST /\allowbreak{}executions/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}addenda。签署校验医生本人及版本。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-13}} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}visits/\allowbreak{}\{visit\allowbreak{}Id\}/\allowbreak{}record/\allowbreak{}sign；POST /\allowbreak{}signed-\allowbreak{}records/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}addenda。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-14}} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}stage-\allowbreak{}tasks/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}appointments；POST /\allowbreak{}handovers/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}decision。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-15}} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}visits/\allowbreak{}\{visit\allowbreak{}Id\}/\allowbreak{}invoice/\allowbreak{}prepare；GET /\allowbreak{}visits/\allowbreak{}\{visit\allowbreak{}Id\}/\allowbreak{}invoice。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-16}} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}receipts；POST /\allowbreak{}receipts/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}confirm；POST /\allowbreak{}receipts/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}allocations。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-17}} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}corrections；POST /\allowbreak{}corrections/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}approval；POST /\allowbreak{}refunds/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}verified-\allowbreak{}result。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-18}} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}daily-\allowbreak{}closes/\allowbreak{}prepare；POST /\allowbreak{}daily-\allowbreak{}closes/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}review；POST /\allowbreak{}interclinic-\allowbreak{}items/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}confirm。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-19}} & \Cell{GET/\allowbreak{}POST /\allowbreak{}compensation/\allowbreak{}months；PUT /\allowbreak{}compensation/\allowbreak{}months/\allowbreak{}\{id\}。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-20}} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}compensation/\allowbreak{}months/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}close；POST /\allowbreak{}compensation/\allowbreak{}months/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}reopen。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-21}} & \Cell{GET/\allowbreak{}POST /\allowbreak{}compensation/\allowbreak{}quarters；GET /\allowbreak{}compensation/\allowbreak{}quarters/\allowbreak{}\{id\}。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-22}} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}compensation/\allowbreak{}quarters/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}submit、/\allowbreak{}approve、/\allowbreak{}void；POST /\allowbreak{}compensation/\allowbreak{}allocations/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}payments；POST /\allowbreak{}compensation/\allowbreak{}payments/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}resolve；GET /\allowbreak{}compensation/\allowbreak{}me。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-23}} & \Cell{GET /\allowbreak{}reports/\allowbreak{}appointments 按门诊、医生和日期查看预约及原因统计；GET /\allowbreak{}shifts 查看已发布排班。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-24}} & \Cell{GET、POST /\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}accounts 查询与创建员工账号；PUT /\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}accounts/\allowbreak{}\{id\} 更新账号资料与授权；POST /\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}accounts/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}reset-password 重置临时密码；POST /\allowbreak{}auth/\allowbreak{}change-password 修改本人密码。}\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}
}
\subsection{品牌官网与预约入口}\label{sec_public_site}
首页按首屏、品牌介绍、诊疗服务、门诊环境、医生团队、预约指引和页脚排列。首屏双栏呈现标题与门诊视频，预约为主按钮，探索服务为次按钮。品牌与门诊环境段用接待区、诊疗室及照护细节图片连接；服务卡片进入目录详情；医生团队由公开医生列表生成。窄屏将双栏排为上下阅读顺序。\par
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{3.94cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{6.24cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{4.94cm}@{}}
\caption{官网页面与数据绑定}\label{tab_public_routes}\\
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 入口} & \Cell{\bfseries 页面与操作} & \Cell{\bfseries 数据来源}\\\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 入口} & \Cell{\bfseries 页面与操作} & \Cell{\bfseries 数据来源}\\\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{{/\allowbreak{}}} & \Cell{首屏、品牌介绍、诊疗服务、门诊环境、医生团队、预约指引与页脚。} & \Cell{品牌场景与视频使用静态素材；门诊、医生、项目目录独立加载。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{{/\allowbreak{}explore/\allowbreak{}services}、{/\allowbreak{}explore/\allowbreak{}services/\allowbreak{}\{id\}}} & \Cell{服务列表与详情显示简介、预约时长及相关服务，详情可跳预约指引。} & \Cell{GET {/\allowbreak{}services}；按项目名称匹配场景图。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{{/\allowbreak{}explore/\allowbreak{}doctors}、{/\allowbreak{}explore/\allowbreak{}doctors/\allowbreak{}\{id\}}} & \Cell{显示姓名、专业方向、介绍、擅长及出诊门诊；虚构医生头像带示意标记。} & \Cell{GET {/\allowbreak{}doctors} 与 {/\allowbreak{}clinics}；示意头像由前端图集提供。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{{/\allowbreak{}explore/\allowbreak{}clinics}、{/\allowbreak{}explore/\allowbreak{}clinics/\allowbreak{}\{id\}}} & \Cell{显示门诊照片、简介、地址、电话、邮箱、营业时间和排班医生。} & \Cell{GET {/\allowbreak{}clinics} 与 {/\allowbreak{}doctors}；按医生的门诊标识关联。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{{/\allowbreak{}\#booking}、{/\allowbreak{}login}} & \Cell{预约入口滚动到扫码指引；预约区与页脚共用微信小程序码，点图可查看大图。员工入口进入账号登录页。} & \Cell{小程序码作为站点静态资源随网页发布。}\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}
}
网站采用暖白、深灰和浅金，标签页使用深灰底的金色微笑弧线图标。首屏提供静态封面和可暂停的静音视频。页面隐藏、视频离开视口或用户选择减少动态效果时暂停，视频不可用时保留静态封面。滚动进入时显示各章节内容，动效遵循系统的减少动态效果设置。导航支持触屏和键盘；目录分别提示加载、失败与空列表，详情标识无效时提示返回目录。\par
\subsection{患者小程序}\label{sec_mini_design}
小程序采用蓝白配色，页面背景为淡蓝白（{\#F5F8FF}），卡片为白色，主要按钮及选中导航用蓝色（{\#2563EB}）。正文采用系统中文无衬线字体，正文28 rpx、页面标题44 rpx，卡片内边距32 rpx。底栏依次为首页、门诊、预约、我的；二级页面使用微信原生返回。访客可浏览公开目录及查询号源，选择时段办理预约、管理本人预约与维护资料须登录。\par
\begin{figure}[H]
\centering
\setlength{\fboxsep}{7pt}

{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{3.04cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{6.84cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.24cm}@{}}
\caption{患者端页面与操作（路由为 pages/页面标识/index）}\label{tab712}\\
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 页面} & \Cell{\bfseries 信息与操作} & \Cell{\bfseries 状态与反馈}\\\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 页面} & \Cell{\bfseries 信息与操作} & \Cell{\bfseries 状态与反馈}\\\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{首页、门诊与医生\newline home、clinics、doctors、doctor-detail} & \Cell{首页放置主预约按钮、门诊、医生团队、项目和科普。门诊介绍在列表展开，可复制地址和拨号；查看医生与号源时带入门诊。医生详情显示擅长、出诊门诊和预约入口。} & \Cell{目录加载失败时可重试。门诊地址可复制；演示门诊标明示例资料。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{参考价格与口腔科普\newline prices、education} & \Cell{演示价格资料按项目列出区间、单位、年份及来源。科普按常见原因、日常预防和诊后护理组织，文章可复制参考来源。} & \Cell{预约页显示项目参考费用，实际项目和收费在门诊确认。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{登录、注册与档案关联\newline login} & \Cell{使用患者账号和密码登录。首次注册建档；已有线下档案时输入前台凭证及出生日期，绑定原患者编号。} & \Cell{凭证过期、已使用或资料不符时提示联系前台重新核对。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{预约与确认\newline booking、confirm} & \Cell{先选择门诊及项目，再选医生、日期与时段；确认页展示就诊人、项目、时间和临时保留倒计时。} & \Cell{加载、失败、无号源分开显示。占位过期或冲突时回到选时段。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{我的预约\newline appointments、detail} & \Cell{列表与详情展示预约编号、项目、医生、门诊和状态；有效预约可填写原因后改期或取消。} & \Cell{按全部、已确认、已取消、已改期筛选。确认后在详情页展示成功反馈；改期后旧详情可打开替代预约。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{我的与隐私协议\newline profile、privacy} & \Cell{展示本人资料，允许修改、保存、选择或移除头像。协议单独成页；账号区提供撤回隐私同意及退出登录。} & \Cell{保存成功后回到资料浏览状态；版本冲突时提示重新读取。退出后清理本机的本人资料、头像及预约状态。}\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}
}

日期页提供七日快选和日历，查询范围由预约策略接口返回。小程序将号源按北京时间分组；服务端根据已发布班次、项目时长、清洁缓冲和患者、医生、牙椅的占用计算可约时段。筛选条件变更后只接收最新请求的结果。\par
选中时段后取得短时占位。确认页显示就诊人、医生、门诊、时段与参考费，勾选就诊人核对及预约规则后提交。提交成功后跳转详情并显示预约编号。改期先占用新时段，事务内创建替代预约并释放旧时段；新时段提交失败时保留原预约。取消、改期或确认的响应丢失时，客户端保留原操作标识，查询回执或使用同一标识重试。\par
\subsubsection{档案关联、隐私与头像}
登录页提供账号登录、新建档案注册和关联已有档案。课程演示可快捷填入患者A（patient.a）或患者B（patient.b）的账号，体验者按页面提示手动填写密码。新账号使用3至80位字母、数字、点、下划线或连字符，密码至少12位。首次注册将账号、患者档案和隐私同意一并保存。关联已有档案时，前台确认身份并发放有效期10分钟的一次性凭证；服务端锁定档案和凭证，验证出生日期，创建账号、保存同意并标记凭证已使用。重新签发时撤销该档案未使用的旧凭证。\par
隐私协议展示处理方、联系方式、用途、保存期限和版本。患者分别确认就诊资料及健康信息的用途。未满14周岁时记录监护人姓名、关系和同意。有效同意按患者、账号和协议版本核对，同一版本适用于连锁门诊。撤回后公开目录仍可浏览，本人预约和资料操作需重新取得同意。\par
头像由患者自行选择、更换或移除。服务端按会话中的患者标识读取及保存头像，校验 JPEG 或 PNG 文件内容和大小，上限为90 KiB。头像数据单独存放，读取响应禁止缓存，操作审计记录修改或移除事件。\par
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{2.74cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.54cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{6.84cm}@{}}
\caption{账号、隐私与头像接口}\label{tab_patient_account_api}\\
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 操作} & \Cell{\bfseries 接口} & \Cell{\bfseries 行为}\\\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 操作} & \Cell{\bfseries 接口} & \Cell{\bfseries 行为}\\\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{登录与退出} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}auth/\allowbreak{}login；POST /\allowbreak{}auth/\allowbreak{}logout} & \Cell{按患者端或员工端签发会话；退出时撤销当前会话。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{就诊资料} & \Cell{GET、PUT /\allowbreak{}patients/\allowbreak{}me} & \Cell{读取本人资料。修改携带版本，服务端锁定档案后核对版本、保存资料与过敏项并返回新版本。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{隐私指引} & \Cell{GET /\allowbreak{}privacy/\allowbreak{}notice；GET /\allowbreak{}privacy/\allowbreak{}status} & \Cell{公开读取指引；登录后核对本人是否已有当前版本的有效同意。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{确认或撤回} & \Cell{POST /\allowbreak{}privacy/\allowbreak{}consent；POST /\allowbreak{}privacy/\allowbreak{}withdraw} & \Cell{保存告知版本和分项同意；撤回时给有效记录写入撤回时间。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{头像管理} & \Cell{GET、PUT /\allowbreak{}patients/\allowbreak{}me/\allowbreak{}avatar} & \Cell{读取头像类型和内容。提交 Base64 内容更换头像，提交空值移除头像。}\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}
}
\subsection{员工工作台与组件}\label{sec_staff_design}
员工登录页在桌面以诊所场景和表单双栏呈现，窄屏以表单为先。演示版本显示总部、店长、前台、医生四个账号（hq、manager.a、front.a、doctor.a）及统一演示密码，点击角色只填入账号，提交时仍校验账号、密码和员工身份。员工会话恢复后从预约总览进入工作台；带有站内返回地址的登录回到原页面。临时密码账号先进入修改密码页。\par
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{3.74cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.94cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.44cm}@{}}
\caption{员工页面、接口与主要操作}\label{tab_staff_routes}\\
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 入口} & \Cell{\bfseries 查询或操作接口} & \Cell{\bfseries 页面行为}\\\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 入口} & \Cell{\bfseries 查询或操作接口} & \Cell{\bfseries 页面行为}\\\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{{/\allowbreak{}login}、{/\allowbreak{}change-\allowbreak{}password}} & \Cell{POST {/\allowbreak{}auth/\allowbreak{}login}、{/\allowbreak{}auth/\allowbreak{}change-\allowbreak{}password}；GET {/\allowbreak{}me}。} & \Cell{登录失败保留账号并显示原因。密码修改后保留当前会话，撤销其他会话。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{{/\allowbreak{}appointments}} & \Cell{GET {/\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}appointments}、{/\allowbreak{}appointments/\allowbreak{}\{id\}}；POST {/\allowbreak{}appointments/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}changes}。} & \Cell{日期导航、分页列表、按医生分组的日程和详情抽屉。仅授权员工可办理改期、取消。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{{/\allowbreak{}shifts}} & \Cell{GET {/\allowbreak{}shifts}；POST {/\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}shifts}、{/\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}shifts/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}cancel}。} & \Cell{按门诊和日期查看班次，日程按医生分组。发布时选择门诊、医生、时段和项目；撤销时带版本。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{{/\allowbreak{}patients}} & \Cell{GET、POST {/\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}patients}；POST {/\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}patients/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}invitations}。} & \Cell{搜索、核对、新建档案与签发关联凭证；选择患者进入代约。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{{/\allowbreak{}booking}} & \Cell{GET {/\allowbreak{}booking-\allowbreak{}policy}、{/\allowbreak{}booking-\allowbreak{}slots}；POST {/\allowbreak{}booking-\allowbreak{}holds}、{/\allowbreak{}appointments/\allowbreak{}confirm}。} & \Cell{核对患者后选门诊、项目、医生与时段，按占位倒计时确认。改期带原预约编号及版本。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{{/\allowbreak{}resources/\allowbreak{}\{kind\}}} & \Cell{读取目录；POST {/\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}\{kind\}}；PUT {/\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}\{kind\}/\allowbreak{}\{id\}}。} & \Cell{按门诊维护门诊、医生、服务、牙椅及资质。编辑已有记录时提交版本。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{{/\allowbreak{}compensation}} & \Cell{GET {/\allowbreak{}compensation/\allowbreak{}context}、{/\allowbreak{}compensation/\allowbreak{}months}、{/\allowbreak{}compensation/\allowbreak{}quarters}、{/\allowbreak{}compensation/\allowbreak{}me}。} & \Cell{总部查看季度和来源，店长查看授权门诊月份，医生查看本人奖金与发放。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{{/\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}accounts}} & \Cell{员工账号使用 {/\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}accounts}，员工档案使用 {/\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}profiles}，均支持查询、新建和更新。} & \Cell{总部维护账号、人员资料及岗位授权，支持停用和重置临时密码。}\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}
}
工作台以深灰侧栏组织预约、排班、患者档案、机构资源、经营和账号管理，浅金强调当前入口。顶栏显示岗位与当前页面，主区以暖白背景和浅边框区分查询、统计和数据表。窄屏采用可收起的导航，表格保留横向浏览；员工可切换紧凑或舒适视图。服务端按账号、岗位和门诊逐次核对访问权限。\par
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{2.24cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{6.34cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{6.54cm}@{}}
\caption{员工岗位与工作入口}\label{tab717}\\
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 岗位} & \Cell{\bfseries 工作入口} & \Cell{\bfseries 操作范围}\\\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 岗位} & \Cell{\bfseries 工作入口} & \Cell{\bfseries 操作范围}\\\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{前台} & \Cell{预约总览、排班、患者档案及代约。} & \Cell{在授权门诊核对患者、建档、签发关联凭证、代约和处理改期取消。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{医生} & \Cell{我的预约、我的排班、本人激励。} & \Cell{查看本人预约与班次，查询已批准的个人份额和发放记录。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{店长} & \Cell{预约、排班、患者服务、门诊资源与经营概览。} & \Cell{在所属门诊组织排班、维护授权资源、查看月度账证与核算进度。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{总部} & \Cell{全门诊预约和资源、员工账号与档案、季度经营。} & \Cell{统一维护目录、员工及岗位，汇总季度来源；制表、独立复核与发放按用例办理。}\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}
}

预约总览进入时选中北京时间当天，可按日期、门诊及状态查询，并在分页列表和按医生分组的单日日程之间切换。跨日预约按开始时间归入当天。详情显示预约编号、时段、门诊、医生、患者和变更事件。列表的预约总数取接口总数，当前页确认数及涉及医生数取当前页记录。日程按所选日期加载多页数据，统计基于已加载记录。页面可见时在一次刷新完成5秒后再刷新，保留日期、筛选、分页和详情；后台标签页及取消提交期间暂停自动刷新。日程加载不完整时标明并提示刷新。\par
前台代约沿用同一号源、占位和命令回执流程。先按姓名或电话核对患者，列表展示脱敏电话、出生年份及账号关联状态，再选择门诊、项目、医生及时间。排班发布后，小程序和员工端均可从号源查询看到新时段。\par
资源页按门诊组织医生、项目和牙椅；门诊公开信息可维护简介和营业时间。医生特长用于展示，发布班次时默认选中门诊项目。系统根据门诊、排班和占用返回时段，界面按加载、无结果和失败分别提示。\par
经营页面按季度和授权门诊筛选。经营总览从已关闭月份生成收入、用品成本、运营费用及净利润的指标、月度趋势和门诊对照。月度底稿按门诊与月份显示缺失、草稿和已关闭，底稿录入医生、三项金额和对应凭据编号。总部另有季度页，详情分个人分配与发放、医生门诊明细和月份来源。医生从“我的激励”查看已批准的个人份额、已发金额及余额。\par
总部维护员工账号与档案、所属门诊和岗位。员工使用临时密码登录后先设置新密码；变更员工账号授权或重置密码时撤销其会话。员工自行修改密码后保留当前会话，注销其他会话。\par
\Needspace{208pt}
\subsection{预约两端共用的数据契约}\label{sec_booking_client_contract}
门诊、医生、牙椅、项目和班次使用 UUID，时间点以带时区的 ISO 日期时间传输，界面按北京时间显示。服务端按班次内有效时段、项目时长及缓冲计算候选，过滤封锁牙椅、过期时段和资源重叠。班次应覆盖项目且门诊开放该项目，医生特长仅用于介绍。默认以15分钟为步长生成候选，每名医生同一开始时间返回一张可用牙椅，单次至多500条。跨门诊排班的默认转场间隔为30分钟。\par
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{3.04cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{6.34cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{5.74cm}@{}}
\caption{号源、占位及预约的输入和结果}\label{tab_booking_client_contract}\\
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 操作} & \Cell{\bfseries 关键输入} & \Cell{\bfseries 结果与页面动作}\\\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\Cell{\bfseries 操作} & \Cell{\bfseries 关键输入} & \Cell{\bfseries 结果与页面动作}\\\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{查询日期及号源} & \Cell{GET {/\allowbreak{}booking-\allowbreak{}policy} 取得可查起止日期。GET {/\allowbreak{}booking-\allowbreak{}slots} 输入 {clinicId}、{serviceId}、{date}，可选 {doctorId}。} & \Cell{返回医生、牙椅、班次、开始时间、服务结束、占用截止及预约参考价。仅显示未来时段。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{占位} & \Cell{POST {/\allowbreak{}booking-\allowbreak{}holds} 提交门诊、项目、医生、牙椅、班次及开始时间，员工另传患者编号。} & \Cell{返回占位编号、版本、到期时间、号源和规则。默认保留300秒。到期后重新选择。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{确认} & \Cell{POST {/\allowbreak{}appointments/\allowbreak{}confirm} 输入 {holdId}、{holdVersion}、{patientConfirmed} 与 {rulesAccepted}（均为真），并发送幂等键。} & \Cell{同一事务转为已约占用并生成预约编号、事件和回执。两端以相同预约标识查询。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{改期或取消} & \Cell{POST {/\allowbreak{}appointments/\allowbreak{}\{id\}/\allowbreak{}changes} 提交 {intent}、{reason}、{expectedVersion}；改期另传新占位编号及版本。} & \Cell{成功后释放旧占用；改期返回新预约并保留新旧关联。办理时限取原预约规则，默认提前120分钟。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{清单与回执} & \Cell{患者 GET {/\allowbreak{}appointments}，员工 GET {/\allowbreak{}staff/\allowbreak{}appointments}；详情 GET {/\allowbreak{}appointments/\allowbreak{}\{id\}}。结果待确认时 GET {/\allowbreak{}commands/\allowbreak{}\{key\}}。} & \Cell{清单按开始时间倒序，分页默认20条、上限100条。回执只向原账号返回；未找到结果时可用原键重试。}\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}
}
冲突或版本变化时刷新原记录和号源，界面保留当前选择条件；网络超时且提交结果未知时，暂存同一账号的原请求和命令标识。患者退出后清除会话和已呈现的本人资料，待确认请求只允许原账号恢复。\par

\section{质量要求与验收设计}
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\begin{longtable}{@{}p{1.440cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{2.940cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{10.740cm}@{}}
\caption{全局业务规则}\label{tab723}\\
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{编号} & \bfseries \Cell{主题} & \bfseries \Cell{业务处理规则}\\\midrule
\endfirsthead
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{编号} & \bfseries \Cell{主题} & \bfseries \Cell{业务处理规则}\\\midrule
\endhead
\bottomrule\endlastfoot
\Cell{\mbox{BR01}} & \Cell{患者档案} & \Cell{同一患者跨门诊沿用同一编号；家庭成员分别建档。前台核对姓名、联系方式等资料后处理疑似重复档案。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR02}} & \Cell{过敏核对} & \Cell{患者逐项填写过敏信息，状态分为已知过敏、未报告过敏和情况不详。医生接诊时核对并记录结果。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR03}} & \Cell{医生与排班} & \Cell{按门诊开放项目和班次生成预约候选。医生特长用于选医。同一医生的班次保持时间错开，跨店出诊预留转场时间。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR04}} & \Cell{可预约时段} & \Cell{已发布班次、项目时长、牙椅及已有预约共同决定可约时段。确认时统一锁定资源，成功后生成预约编号。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR05}} & \Cell{改期与取消} & \Cell{患者在小程序选择新时段改期，成功后释放旧时段；取消时填写原因并释放原时段，双方均可查看变更记录。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR06}} & \Cell{到店登记} & \Cell{前台核对预约后记录到店时间。预约状态分别显示待到店、已到店、已取消、已改期和未到店；未到店结果由前台核实。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR07}} & \Cell{治疗方案与增项} & \Cell{医生面诊后说明项目和费用，患者确认后执行。新增治疗项目先说明并取得确认，保存方案版本及确认记录。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR08}} & \Cell{治疗记录与计费} & \Cell{医生记录实际完成的项目、牙位、数量和单价。前台根据当次治疗明细核对费用，历史明细保留当时价格。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR09}} & \Cell{单次就诊结算} & \Cell{一次就诊使用一张结算单，逐项列出已完成治疗的数量、单价和金额。再次打开该次结算时返回同一单号。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR10}} & \Cell{收入与收款} & \Cell{治疗收入按结算单和费用调整记录汇总；收款按实际到账金额汇总。预付款在完成治疗并结算后计入相应服务收入。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR11}} & \Cell{金额计算} & \Cell{金额按人民币分保存，逐项计算后汇总。分配到各单据、医生或费用对象的金额合计与原金额相等。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR12}} & \Cell{费用更正与退款} & \Cell{费用更正记录原单、原因和调整金额。退款关联原收款，核准后登记实际退回金额及凭据，累计退款以可退余额为限。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR13}} & \Cell{成本归属} & \Cell{用品成本归入实际使用的医生和就诊；运营费用优先直接归属，共同费用按已核对工时分配。每笔凭据只入账一次。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR14}} & \Cell{月度核对} & \Cell{前台整理收退款和日结差异，店长核对用品、运营费用及账证，总部复核后关闭月份。后续更正另存记录并关联原账证。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR15}} & \Cell{季度净利润} & \Cell{医生季度净利润=治疗收入净额-用品成本-运营费用。总部汇总各门诊三个月的明细，个人跨店收入和成本合并计算。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR16}} & \Cell{季度基金} & \Cell{将全体医生季度净利润相加；合计为正时提取10\%，四舍五入到分，合计为零或负数时基金为0。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR17}} & \Cell{个人奖金} & \Cell{按各医生跨店合计后的正净利润占比分配基金。金额先算到分，剩余几分钱依小数余数从大到小补入，同余数按员工号排序；全部奖金合计等于基金。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR18}} & \Cell{复核职责} & \Cell{费用更正、退款、月度关闭和基金审批分别由提交人与另一名获授权人员复核；病历由接诊医生签署。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{BR19}} & \Cell{操作与通知} & \Cell{变更记录操作者、时间、原因及前后内容。业务完成后安排通知；发送失败时重试并交前台跟进。}\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}
}
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}{\textwidth}
{\fontsize{10.5pt}{13.2pt}\selectfont\setlength{\tabcolsep}{0pt}\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.08}
\captionof{table}{质量目标与验证条件}\label{tab724}
\begin{tabular}{@{}p{1.540cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{2.840cm}@{\hspace{0.16cm}}p{10.740cm}@{}}
\toprule
\bfseries \Cell{编号} & \bfseries \Cell{要求} & \bfseries \Cell{验证方式与通过条件}\\\midrule
\Cell{\mbox{QR01}} & \Cell{可用性} & \Cell{前台和患者分别走查预约、改期和付款异常；关键状态及恢复动作可见，不因错误丢失已填资料。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{QR02}} & \Cell{并发一致性} & \Cell{两个请求同时争用一时段；仅一个确认，另一个可重新选择。收款、退款和分配并发不得超额。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{QR03}} & \Cell{性能} & \Cell{以30门诊、100万预约/\allowbreak{}就诊事实的固定生成数据，测试200并发查询；号源和患者历史查询P95目标≤2秒。记录硬件、索引、冷热缓存和失败率，结果未达标则定位查询及容量。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{QR04}} & \Cell{可恢复性} & \Cell{数据库每日全量备份并保存可恢复的增量日志，演练目标RPO≤15分钟、RTO≤4小时；恢复后核对金额及关键关系。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{QR05}} & \Cell{权限与隐私} & \Cell{对患者互访、员工跨店及总部查询临床详情逐项否定测试；越权无数据返回且审计留痕。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{QR06}} & \Cell{审计与维护} & \Cell{每笔受控变更可定位操作者、请求、版本与来源；按用例组织契约测试及变更评审。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{QR07}} & \Cell{扩展性} & \Cell{通过统一配置增加门诊、医生和服务项目；跨店规则及报表使用统一门诊标识。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{QR08}} & \Cell{资金与核算} & \Cell{逐项核对收入、成本、费用和基金金额；用包含盈利、亏损及分配后剩余几分钱的样例复算，个人奖金合计等于基金。}\\[1pt]
\bottomrule\end{tabular}
}\end{minipage}\par\vspace{6pt}
\Needspace{459pt}
\subsection{三端页面的交付验收}
\begin{enumerate}
\item 从官网首页打开医生、门诊与服务详情，再由预约按钮进入扫码区。微信扫描页面的小程序码进入患者首页，官网的员工入口进入登录页。
\item 在小程序用虚构患者登录，选门诊、项目、医生、日期及号源，核对就诊人并确认。员工在授权门诊按就诊日期查询，打开详情，核对预约编号、状态、时间和门诊。
\item 小程序改期后同时核对旧记录和新记录，再取消新记录。员工刷新后能查到相同状态及原因；原资源按规则回到可约范围。
\item 关联已有患者档案后保持原编号。保存资料和更换头像后再次打开个人页；撤回同意后公开目录仍可浏览，受保护操作要求重新确认。
\item 用总部、店长、前台和医生账号分别登录。总部核对跨店数据；店长和前台仅可操作授权门诊；医生仅可查本人预约和排班，且可查看本人批准的激励。
\item 在两家演示门诊各录入一季度的月度账证，分别由不同员工关闭。总部生成季度，核对医生跨店合计、用品成本、运营费用、基金及个人份额，并检查预约参考费用未自动计入收入。
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{端到端业务验收}

\Cell{小程序预约与前台代约} & \Cell{两端同时确认同一医生、门诊及牙椅时段} & \Cell{只有一笔确认成功；另一端提示重新选择时段。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{两端预约联动} & \Cell{员工发布班次；患者确认、改期或取消} & \Cell{小程序查到号源；员工端查看同一预约编号、状态和变更记录。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{本人档案和隐私} & \Cell{用有效凭证关联原档案，保存资料和头像后撤回同意} & \Cell{患者编号保持不变；保存后显示新资料。撤回后需重新同意才可管理预约和资料。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{医生特长与跨店排班} & \Cell{为不同特长医生发布同一门诊项目的班次，再提交时间重叠的跨店班次} & \Cell{查询返回已发布的可约时段。时间重叠时提示冲突，调整后发布。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{增项到结算} & \Cell{原方案一项，诊疗中新增一项} & \Cell{先确认增项再执行，两项均保留单位和单价快照，只形成同次就诊一张单。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{部分收款与退款} & \Cell{500元费用先收300元、补收200元，再批准减免100元并退回100元} & \Cell{应收400元，净到账400元；保留两笔收款、减免和退款凭据。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{跨店代收} & \Cell{甲店治疗，乙店成功收款} & \Cell{服务收入归甲店医生，资金归乙店核对，内部划转不增加患者收入。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{医生季度含亏损} & \Cell{净利润70000、40000、-15000元} & \Cell{总净利润95000元，基金9500元，分配合计9500元。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{历史价格变更} & \Cell{开单后目录价格上调} & \Cell{历史执行和结算保持原单位价，季度重算不使用新价格。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{通知与支付中断} & \Cell{提交成功后通知失败，退款调用超时} & \Cell{预约不撤回；通知形成待办。退款保持待核实，查原交易后再决定重试。}\\[1pt]
\bottomrule\end{tabular}
}\end{minipage}\par\vspace{6pt}
\subsection{部署与维护方案}
演示环境由HTTPS网关、Vue静态站点、Fastify应用及PostgreSQL组成。网页通过同域 {/api/v1} 访问应用；微信小程序发布构建把同一 HTTPS 接口地址写入构建产物，并将域名登记为请求域名。演示员工账号提示由站点构建配置提供，患者端使用两名虚构患者，演示排班及账证按虚构来源标记。数据库只向服务端开放；数据库密码、服务密钥和备份凭证由运行环境提供。附件存储使用受限访问和短时授权，病历、过敏及支付凭据按业务权限读取。\par
官网与工作台共用 Vue 构建产物。部署入口使用 Nginx 将页面路径交给前端路由，将接口请求转发至 Fastify。小程序构建采用 \url{ANDREW_MINI_BUILD=production}，用 \url{ANDREW_MINI_API_URL} 指向 \url{https://andrewdental.top/api/v1}。上线检查应从官网扫码进入小程序，再用员工工作台按预约日期核对提交结果。\par
数据库结构变更使用带版本、校验和与回退说明的迁移。发布前在备份副本验证迁移与恢复，核对外键、单据唯一性、金额合计一致和季度来源版本；失败时停止切换并恢复上一可用环境。监控聚焦请求失败、任务积压、预约冲突率、在途支付时长及备份滞后，告警必须对应处理责任人。\par

\Cell{\mbox{UC-01}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{家庭成员共用电话→生成不同患者编号。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-02}} & \Cell{分析类图、内部序列图、操作、事务、仓储及界面设计} & \Cell{选择门诊和项目→列出相应班次的可约医生，显示各自特长。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-03}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{把单位从次改为牙→建立新版本，旧执行仍按次。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-04}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{同一医生异店班次重叠→拒绝第二次发布。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-05}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{医生空闲但治疗位不可用→不返回该时段。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-06}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{顾客与前台同时确认同资源→仅一个预约成立。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-07}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{抢新时段失败→旧约仍可查询且占用不变。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-08}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{重复释放事件→同一患者不重复收到同轮邀请。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-09}} & \Cell{分析类图、内部序列图、操作、事务、仓储及界面设计} & \Cell{患者小程序预约后到店→前台核对预约并记录到店时间；预约过时未到→核实后登记未到。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-10}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{患者有乳胶过敏但未核对→执行放行受阻并保留评估。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-11}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{新增X光但未取得确认→执行接口拒绝该增项。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-12}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{计划3单位但实际仅做2单位→执行记录2，结算依据为2。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-13}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{两次提交同一版本→只有一个签署号。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-14}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{跨店交接尚未接受→无法确认该阶段预约。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-15}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{并发生成同一就诊账单→同一单号且项目仅计一次。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-16}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{同一支付回执推送三次→一笔收款。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-17}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{多收款退回→现金净额减少，服务收入不变。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-18}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{A店为B店代收→患者一笔收款，双方往来等额反向。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-19}} & \Cell{分析类图、内部序列图、操作、事务、仓储及界面设计} & \Cell{登记某医生月收入、用品成本、运营费用及凭据→保存并可逐笔查看。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-20}} & \Cell{分析类图、内部序列图、操作、事务、仓储及界面设计} & \Cell{制表人提交月份→另一人员核对完整账证并关闭；修订保留原版本。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-21}} & \Cell{分析类图、内部序列图、操作、事务、仓储及界面设计} & \Cell{三个月和全部门诊资料核对完成→汇总医生净利润并提取10\%基金。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-22}} & \Cell{分析类图、内部序列图、操作、事务、仓储及界面设计} & \Cell{另一总部人员批准基金→登记发放凭据；医生查看本人奖金、已发金额和余额。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-23}} & \Cell{分析类图、内部序列图、操作、事务、仓储及界面设计} & \Cell{选择门诊和日期→显示预约、到店、取消、未到数量及对应记录，取消记录附原因。}\\[1pt]
\Cell{\mbox{UC-24}} & \Cell{分析类图；边界—控制—实体序列；操作、仓储、事务及界面契约} & \Cell{店长授总部权限→拒绝且原授权不变。}\\[1pt]
\end{longtable}
}
